}\n    def skipWs = { ->\n        while (state.pos < state.len) {\n            char c = s.charAt(state.pos)\n            if (c == ' ' as char || c == '\\t' as char || c == '\\n' as char || c == '\\r' att : null\n    if (a == null) return null\n    def raw = a.content\n    if (raw instanceof String && raw.trim()) return raw.trim()\n    return null\n}\n\ndef convertAttachment = { att ->\n    def a = (att instanceof Map) ? att : null\n    if (a == null) return \"\"\n\n    def rawContent = getRawContentString(att)\n\n    def contentType = (a.contentType ?: \"\").toString().toLowerCase()\n    def content = parseContent(a.content)\n    def c = (content instanceof Map) ? content : null\n\n    try {\n        if (contentType.contains(\"adaptive\") || (c != null && (c.type == \"AdaptiveCard\" || c.body instanceof List))) {\n            def sb = new StringBuilder()\n\n            boolean isPoll = isPollCard(c)\n\n            if (isPoll) {\n                sb << \"=== POLL ===\\n\"\n            }\n\n            if (c?.body instanceof List) {\n                c.body.each { elem ->\n                    def rendered = renderElement(elem)\n                    if (rendered?.trim()) sb << rendered << \"\\n\"\n                }\n            }\n\n            if (!isPoll && c?.actions instanceof List) {\n                def actionLines = c.actions.collect { act ->\n                    def a2 = (act instanceof Map) ? act : null\n                    a2 ? \"[Action: ${a2.title ?: a2.type ?: 'Unknown'}]\" : \"\"\n                }.findAll { it }\n                if (actionLines) sb << actionLines.join(\"  \") << \"\\n\"\n            }\n\n            def result = sb.toString().trim()\n            if (!result) return rawContent ?: \"\"\n            return result\n        }\n\n        if (contentType.contains(\"hero\") || contentType.contains(\"thumbnail\")) {\n            def sb = new StringBuilder()\n            if (c?.title)    sb << c.title << \"\\n\"\n            if (c?.subtitle) sb << c.subtitle << \"\\n\"\n            if (c?.text)     sb << cleanMarkdown(c.text.toString()) << \"\\n\"\n            def result = sb.toString().trim()\n            if (!result) return rawContent ?: \"\"\n            return result\n        }\n\n        if (contentType.startsWith(\"text/html\")) {\n            def result = htmlToText((a.content ?: content ?: \"\").toString())\n            if (!result?.trim()) return rawContent ?: \"\"\n            return result\n        }\n\n        if (contentType.startsWith(\"text/\")) {\n            def result = (a.content ?: content ?: \"\").toString().trim()\n            if (!result) return rawContent ?: \"\"\n            return result\n        }\n\n        if (a.name || a.contentUrl) return \"File: ${a.name ?: ''}${a.contentUrl ? (att.contentType ?: \"unknown\") : \"unknown\").toString()\n        def attName = ((att instanceof Map) ? (att.name ?: \"\") : \"\").toString().trim()\n\n        def description = \"Attachment ${idx + 1}\"\n        if (ct.contains(\"adaptive\")) {\n            if (text.contains(\"=== POLL ===\")) {\n                description = \"Attachment ${idx + 1}: Poll Card\"\n            } else if (text.contains(\"response\") && (text.contains(\"%\"))) {\n                description = \"Attachment ${idx + 1}: Poll Results Card\"\n            } else {\n                description = \"Attachment ${idx + 1}: Adaptive Card\"\n            }\n        } else if (ct.contains(\"hero\"))      { description = \"Attachment ${idx + 1}: Hero Card\" }\n        else if (ct.contains(\"thumbnail\"))   { description = \"Attachment ${idx + 1}: Thumbnail Card\" }\n        else if (ct.contains(\"text/html\"))   { description = \"Attachment ${idx + 1}: HTML Content\" }\n        else if (ct.startsWith(\"text/\"))     { description = \"Attachment ${idx + 1}: Plain Text\" }\n        else if (attName)                    { description = \"Attachment ${idx + 1}: File - ${attName}\" }\n\n        \"=== ${description} ===\\n${text}\"\n    }\n    .findAll { it?.trim() }\n    .join(\"\\n\\n---\\n\\n\")\n\ndef attachmentTypes = processedList\n    .collect { (it instanceof Map) ? (it.contentType ?: \"unknown\") : \"unknown\" }\n    .groupBy { it }\n    .collect { type, items -> [type: type, count: items.size()] }\n    .sort { a, b -> b.count <=> a.count }\n    .collect { it.type + \": \" + it.count }\n    .join(\", \")\n\ndef rawAttachmentsCount = rawList.size()\n\nreturn [\n    \"attachmentsCount\"    : attachmentsCount,\n    \"attachmentTypes\"     : attachmentTypes,\n    \"attachments\"         : attachmentsText,\n    \"rawAttachmentsCount\" : rawAttachmentsCount,\n    \"rawAttachments\"      : rawList\n]",
